# Fanout report -- module `mem`

_Generated by `tests/Stages/Harish_StageTesting/fanout/scripts/fanout_optimize.py`._

> **Note on `inv_N$`**: as of this report, `lib/STDCells/UniqueLib.v` still wraps `inv1$` (drive ~1) inside `inv_N$`, **not** `bufferHInv16$`. Many recommendations assume the macro will be upgraded. If the macro has already been changed in another branch, ignore this note. Otherwise, either upgrade the macro globally (clears every inverter-driven violation at once) or instantiate `bufferHInv*$` explicitly per violation.

## Summary

- Total VIOLATIONs in check.log: **517**
- In-scope (driver inside `mem`): **159**
- Cross-module (driver outside but module name suggests `mem` loads): **43**
- Tristate-bus mismatches: **0**
- Boundary WARNINGs touching `mem`: **0**
- Over-buffered INFOs in scope: **449**

## In-scope violations (159)

### 1. `out` (fanout=5) — driven by `mux2$`

- **Driver path**: (Everywhere_TOP) → mem_unit(mem_TOP) → u_controller(mem_controller) → u0_fsm(mem_controller_fsm) → mem_ready_o_or(or2_N$_WIDTH1) → g_or2[0].u0(MPS_OR_IN2$) → g0(mux2$)
- **Required tier**: `bufferH16$ / bufferHInv16$`

| # | Option | Added delay | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| **✅ recommended** | Replicate gate × 2 (or duplicate source register × 2) | 0.00 ns | In `<file for mem_controller_fsm>` instantiate `mem_ready_o_or` (a `mux2$`-class gate) 2 times with the same fanin; split loads ~3 per copy. **Cleaner equivalent**: trace upstream to the source register(s) feeding `mem_ready_o_or` and clone *those*; the gate is rebuilt per copy automatically. Either way: **Output side**: per-copy fanout = 3 → no per-copy buffer. **Input side**: each fan-in wire of `mem_ready_o_or` now has +1 extra loads → no input buffer. Total added delay = 0.00 + 0.00 = **0.00 ns**. If the gate's fanin signals were already high-fanout, the cascade may be cheaper than shown (existing buffer reused) -- worth inspecting `<file for mem_controller_fsm>`. |
| alt 1 | Buffer with bufferHInv16$ (polarity flip needed) | 0.15 ns | Use `bufferHInv16$` (inverting, **0.15 ns**) only if every consumer of `mem_ready_o_or.out` can absorb the inversion. Pair cost = 0.30 ns. |
| alt 2 | Buffer with bufferH16$ | 0.24 ns | Insert `bufferH16$` after `mem_ready_o_or.out` in `<file for mem_controller_fsm>`; routes all 5 loads through one buffer. The buffer's input is a fanout-1 load on `mem_ready_o_or.out` (no upstream cascade). Adds **0.24 ns** to every load path. |

### 2. `q[7]` (fanout=8) — driven by `reg64e$`

- **Driver path**: (Everywhere_TOP) → mem_unit(mem_TOP) → u_controller(mem_controller) → g_bg_wb[7].u_b15(MPS_reg_rst_we$_WIDTH8) → gen_reg64[0].u_reg(reg64e$)
- **Required tier**: `bufferH16$ / bufferHInv16$`

| # | Option | Added delay | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| **✅ recommended** | Register duplication × 2 | 0.00 ns | In `<file for mem_controller>` clone `g_bg_wb[7].u_b15` into 2 copies (`g_bg_wb[7].u_b15_a` … `g_bg_wb[7].u_b15_b`); each copy drives ~4 loads. **Output side**: per-copy fanout = 4 → no per-copy buffer. **Input side (D)**: fanout = 2 on the next-state wire → no input buffer. clk/rst are clock-tree distributed so they don't count. Total added delay on a load path = 0.00 + 0.00 = **0.00 ns**. This assumes D was fanout-1 before duplication; if the next-state expression already feeds other loads, the input-side cost may be smaller (existing buffer reused) or larger (extra cascade). |
| alt 1 | Buffer with bufferHInv16$ (polarity flip needed) | 0.15 ns | Use `bufferHInv16$` (single inverter, **0.15 ns**) only if every downstream consumer of `g_bg_wb[7].u_b15.q` can absorb the inversion (NAND↔AND swap, mux input swap, etc.). Otherwise pair-cost = 0.30 ns and `bufferH16$` is cheaper. |
| alt 2 | Buffer with bufferH16$ | 0.24 ns | Insert `bufferH16$` between `g_bg_wb[7].u_b15.q` and its loads in `<file for mem_controller>`. The buffer's input is a single load on `g_bg_wb[7].u_b15.q` (fanout-1, no upstream cascade). Adds **0.24 ns** on every path through this signal. |
